Applied to an open-source agent-memory testbed across 5 tags x 3 embedders x 5 collision degrees with paired-bootstrap 95% CIs, the protocol reveals a two-axis pattern: a 256-d hash trigram helps only on closed-vocabulary lexical tags at deep collision; MiniLM-384 dominates both axes; and a 2.7x-parameter BGE-large does not uniformly improve on MiniLM -- it wins on intent-style queries but loses on lexical ones. Encoder capacity alone is not the binding constraint. The synthetic intent-tag null replicates on LongMemEval (n=500) as a single-session-preference recall cliff. Adaptive vector-weight routing on LoCoMo is a measured null: 11.7pp of oracle headroom exists, but no signal we tested recovers it. All 26 result tables and 37 reproduce scripts are version-controlled and verified by a public registry; the protocol is exercised on a deterministically governed memory testbed (event-sourced decision log, DAG-state-machine schema lifecycle) so every reported CI is reproducible byte-for-byte from the ingest stream.

</details>


### [148] [Predicting Causal Effects from Natural Language Queries using Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29631)
*Giuliano Martinelli,Piriyakorn Piriyatamwong,Abelardo Carlos Martinez Lorenzo,Jasmin Baier,Riccardo Orlando,Satvik Garg,Sharif Kazemi,Linxi Wang,Arianna Legovini,Samuel Fraiberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Query2Effect基准（含72,000+自然语言问题与实验描述对齐），并设计了一个两阶段框架（先生成查询的结构化表示，再用监督编码模型预测效应量），实验表明微调相比直接提示LLM可将绝对误差降低27%-71%，且两阶段框架有利于跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验成本高昂且耗时，激发了对利用已有实验证据预测因果效应的兴趣。大语言模型在知识密集型任务上表现优异，但能否用于预测因果效应量尚不清楚。

Method: 构建了Query2Effect基准（72,000+问题与实验描述对齐），提出两阶段框架：第一阶段生成查询的合成结构化表示，第二阶段使用监督编码器模型预测效应量。通过改变查询的内隐性、抽象性和模糊性模拟现实信息检索场景。

Result: 微调后的模型相比开箱即用的提示LLM，绝对误差降低了27%-71%。两阶段框架在跨域泛化上表现更优。

Conclusion: 微调对于因果效应量预测至关重要；将语义解释与数值效应估计分离的两阶段方法有助于提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials are a cornerstone of medicine and the social sciences as they enable reliable estimates of causal effects. However, they are costly and time-consuming to conduct, motivating interest in predicting causal effects from existing experimental evidence. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on knowledge-intensive tasks, raising the question of whether these models can be used for forecasting causal effect sizes. To investigate this, we introduce Query2Effect, a new large-scale benchmark consisting of more than 72,000 natural language questions aligned with experiment descriptions, created to simulate realistic information-seeking scenarios by varying query specificity along dimensions of implicitness, abstraction, and ambiguity. We then propose a two-step framework that first generates a synthetic structured representation of a query before predicting effect size using a supervised encoder model. Experiments show that finetuning plays a crucial role in improving prediction performance, with absolute error reducing by -27% up to -71% compared to prompted out-of-the-box LLMs, and that our two-step framework is beneficial for out-of-domain generalization, highlighting the benefits of separating semantic interpretation from numerical effect estimation.

</details>


### [149] [Evaluating Cross-lingual Knowledge Consistency in Code-Mixed vis-a-vis Indian Languages using IndicKLAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29637)
*Debajyoti Mazumder,Divyansh Pathak,Prashant Kodali,Aditya Joshi,Akshay Agarwal,Jasabanta Patro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建IndiKLAR基准，覆盖18种印度语言及11种代码混合变体，发现大语言模型在印度语言上知识召回准确率比英语低约50%，但代码混合输入可将差距缩小至5%以内。研究进一步揭示从原生语言→代码混合→英语的性能轨迹中存在一致的"翻转点"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在英语上知识召回可靠，但在低资源语言（尤其是印度语言及其代码混合变体）上表现严重退化，这一跨语言一致性差距尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 构建IndiKLAR基准（覆盖18种印度语言，其中11种配对代码混合变体，经母语者验证）；评估9个开源模型；比较多种提示策略（两阶段翻译后回答、单阶段联合翻译与回答、Translate-in-Thought）。

Result: 原生语言准确率差距可达~0.50；代码混合输入将差距缩小至~0.05；识别出从原生语言到代码混合到英语性能轨迹中的一致翻转点。

Conclusion: 代码混合输入无需模型干预即可大幅缩小跨语言知识召回差距；翻转点的发现表明存在一个从错误到正确预测的临界边界，该边界既可由输入表面形式诱导，也可由模型内部转换过程诱导。

Abstract: Large language models recall knowledge reliably in English but often fail on the same query posed in a lower-resourced language -- a crosslingual consistency gap that remains underexplored for Indian languages and their code-mixed counterparts. To study this gap, we introduce IndiKLAR, an Indic extension of the KLAR-CLC benchmark covering 18 of the 22 scheduled Indian languages and pairing them with code-mixed variants for 11 widely used language pairs, with native-speaker verification of both monolingual and code-mixed variants for these 11 settings. This three-way alignment offers a unique opportunity to examine how knowledge recall consistency varies across the spectrum of English, code-mixed, and native Indian language inputs. Evaluating across nine open-weight models, we find that the native-language accuracy gap to English can reach $\sim$0.50, while code-mixed inputs close most of it -- bringing performance within $\sim$0.05 of English without any model-level intervention. Motivated by this, we evaluate several prompting strategies that vary in how language conversion is exposed, including a two-stage translate-then-answer setup, a one-stage joint translation-and-answer prompt, and Translate-in-Thought (TinT) -- a single-step strategy in which the model converts the input internally and emits only the final answer. Across the performance trajectory native $\rightarrow$ code-mixed $\rightarrow$ English, we identify a consistent flip point -- the boundary between incorrect and correct prediction -- that lies between the native and code-mixed settings. Interestingly, this holds whether the trajectory is induced by the input surface form or by the model's internal conversion process.

</details>


### [150] [Verifiable Rewards Beyond Math and Code: Lightweight Corpus-Grounded Process Supervision for Factual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29648)
*Shicheng Fan,Haochang Hao,Dehai Min,Weihao Liu,Philip S. Yu,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CorVer，一种基于语料库（Wikipedia共现统计）的轻量级过程奖励方法，用于强化学习提升知识密集型问答的事实准确性。它替代了昂贵的神经验证器，在30个（模型、基准）组合上全部优于原始基线，平均TriviaQA提升+4.1个百分点，训练速度快4.8-8.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型问答中应用强化学习提升事实准确性面临奖励设计困境：响应级奖励太粗粒度无法区分推理轨迹中的正确与错误陈述；句子级奖励虽更细粒度但依赖NLI验证器、LLM评判或知识验证管道，部署成本高且在稀有实体事实中不可靠。

Method: 提出CorVer（Corpus Verify），一种即插即用的过程奖励方法。它用Wikipedia共现统计替代神经验证器，基于语料库生成信号。具体包括：1）句子级信用分配；2）通过简单对齐将句子级信用映射为token级优势。仅需0.5B的提取器和每次句子一次语料库查找。

Result: 在30个（模型、基准）组合（覆盖6种指令微调模型3B-14B和5个QA基准）上，CorVer在所有组合上均优于原始基线，TriviaQA平均提升+4.1个百分点。在20个可行配置的细胞中，有18个优于四种神经验证器基线，训练速度快4.8-8.4倍。

Conclusion: CorVer作为一种轻量级、即插即用的过程奖励方法，通过语料库共现统计有效解决了RL奖励设计的困境，在提升事实准确性的同时大幅降低了计算开销，特别适用于稀有实体知识场景。

Abstract: Applying reinforcement learning to improve factual accuracy in knowledge-intensive question answering faces a reward design dilemma. Response-level rewards provide only coarse supervision and cannot distinguish correct from incorrect statements within a reasoning trace. Sentence-level alternatives offer finer-grained feedback, but typically rely on NLI verifiers, LLM judges, or knowledge-verification pipelines that are expensive to deploy at RL scale and often unreliable for rare-entity facts, where accurate reward signals are especially important. We propose CorVer (Corpus Verify), a lightweight, plug-in-ready process reward that replaces neural verifiers with a corpus-grounded signal derived from Wikipedia co-occurrence statistics. CorVer assigns sentence-level credit and maps it to token-level advantages via a simple alignment, requiring only a 0.5B extractor and a single corpus lookup per sentence. Across 30 (model, benchmark) cells spanning six instruction-tuned models (3B to 14B) and five QA benchmarks, CorVer improves over the raw baseline for every cell, with an average TriviaQA gain of +4.1 pp. It also outperforms four neural-verifier baselines in 18 of 20 cells under their feasible configurations, while training 4.8 to 8.4x faster.

</details>


### [151] [EviLink: Multi-Path Schema Linking with Uncertainty-Guided Evidence Acquisition for Large-Scale Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29670)
*Huawei Zheng,Sen Yang,Zhaorui Yang,Yuhui Zhang,Haozhe Feng,Haoxuan Li,Xuan Yi,Chao Hu,Defeng Xie,Chen Hou,Danqing Huang,Wei Chen,Yingcai Wu,Peng Chen,Dazhen Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大规模Text-to-SQL中的schema链接重新定义为不确定性感知的模式需求推理，并实例化为EviLink系统，通过多假设模式基础与不确定性引导的证据获取，在BIRD-Dev和Spider2-Snow上取得了更好的模式完整性、相关性和token成本平衡，在Spider2-Snow上实现了90.15%的字段级严格召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有schema链接方法通常将问题视为围绕单一SQL路径的确定性选择，但复杂问题可能存在多种有效实现方式，需要不同的模式支持。因此需要一种能够处理多路径不确定性、区分必需与路径依赖的模式项，并有针对性地获取证据的新方法。

Method: 将schema链接重新框架为不确定性感知的模式需求推理：系统在多个可能的SQL路径上区分必需的schema项与路径依赖的不确定项，仅在需要时获取证据。具体实例化为EviLink，结合多假设模式基础（multi-hypothesis schema grounding）与不确定性引导的证据获取（uncertainty-guided evidence acquisition）。

Result: 在BIRD-Dev和Spider2-Snow数据集上实验，EviLink在模式完整性、相关性和token成本之间取得了更好的平衡。在Spider2-Snow上达到90.15%的字段级严格召回率，平均使用123.30K tokens，并在固定生成器下提升了下游SQL生成质量。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的schema链接方法优于传统的确定性选择方法，能够更有效地处理大规模数据库中的多路径歧义性问题，在保持高召回率的同时控制token成本，并能提升下游SQL生成性能。

Abstract: Schema linking is a difficult and important step in large-scale Text-to-SQL, where systems must identify a compact yet sufficient schema context from large and ambiguous databases. Existing methods often treat schema linking as deterministic selection around a single SQL path, but complex questions may admit multiple valid realizations with different schema needs. We reframe schema linking as uncertainty-aware schema-need inference over multiple plausible SQL paths, where the system distinguishes required schema items from path-dependent uncertain ones and acquires evidence only where needed. We instantiate this reframing with EviLink, which combines multi-hypothesis schema grounding with uncertainty-guided evidence acquisition. Experiments on BIRD-Dev and Spider2-Snow show that this perspective improves the balance among schema completeness, schema relevance, and token cost. On Spider2-Snow, EviLink achieves 90.15% field-level strict recall rate, uses 123.30K average tokens, and improves downstream SQL generation under a fixed generator.

</details>


### [152] [Spurious Prompts: Can Irrelevant Prompts Steer Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29678)
*Pawel Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Jerzy Sarosiek,Przemyslaw Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现与任务语义无关的提示（spurious prompts）能够显著影响大语言模型的行为，并通过黑盒搜索方法发现这类提示可提升模型性能，甚至匹配或超越标准提示方法和任务感知的提示优化。同时，这些虚假提示也能将模型引导至非预期行为（如固定选择第一个选项、输出特定数字等），揭示了一种新型的提示敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注与任务相关的指令、示例或推理线索对模型行为的影响，而忽略了与任务语义无关的提示是否也能影响模型行为。本文旨在探索这种被忽视的提示敏感性，揭示LLM在何种程度上会被任务无关的虚假提示所引导。

Method: 提出一种黑盒搜索过程用于发现虚假提示；在推理和问答基准上使用0.8B到27B参数的三个模型家族进行实验；通过对比标准提示基线、任务感知提示优化等方法评估虚假提示的效果。

Result: 虚假提示能够提升模型性能，常匹配或超越标准提示基线和任务感知提示优化；同时能引导模型产生非预期行为，如反复选择第一个答案选项、产生错误答案、输出特定类型数字（偶数、质数、小数）等。

Conclusion: 揭示了一种新型的提示敏感性：LLM可被与待解决任务无关的提示系统性引导。这提示我们需要更全面地理解提示对模型行为的影响机制。

Abstract: Large language models are highly sensitive to prompts, but this sensitivity is usually studied through task-relevant instructions, demonstrations, or reasoning cues. In this paper, we study a different form of prompt sensitivity: whether prompts that are semantically unrelated to the task can nevertheless steer model behavior. We call them spurious prompts and show their surprising efficacy. We also propose a simple black-box search procedure for discovering them. Across reasoning and question-answering benchmarks, using models ranging from 0.8B to 27B parameters and spanning three model families, we show that spurious prompts can improve performance, often matching or outperforming standard prompting baselines and task-aware prompt optimization. We further show that they can steer models toward unintended behaviors, such as repeatedly selecting the first answer option, producing incorrect answers, returning an even, prime or small number without explicitly instructing the model to do so. These findings reveal a new kind of prompt sensitivity: LLMs can be systematically steered by prompts that are unrelated to the task they are asked to solve. Our code is available at https://github.com/Batorskq/spurious

</details>


### [153] [Scaling Laws for Agent Harnesses via Effective Feedback Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29682)
*Xuanliang Zhang,Dingzirui Wang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出有效反馈计算量（EFC），一种追踪级别的缩放坐标，仅当反馈具备信息性、有效性、非冗余性且被保留用于后续决策时才予以计入，并以任务需求归一化。实验表明，EFC比原始计算量（token、工具调用等）及多变量SAS基线更能预测失败率（R²最高达0.99），证明智能体系统的性能缩放更多取决于原始预算转化为有效反馈的效率，而非总计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前测试时缩放分析通常以原始支出（token数、工具调用次数、操作数、耗时或成本）为参数，无法区分有用反馈与冗余或不稳定交互，缺乏能真正衡量反馈质量的缩放坐标。

Method: 提出EFC（Effective Feedback Compute），在追踪层面仅当反馈满足信息性、有效性、非冗余性且被保留用于后续决策时才计入；并以任务需求D_task归一化（EFC/D_task）。在合成可控任务、可执行代码任务、真实基准追踪、保留集及前瞻验证批上进行对比实验。

Result: 原始token和工具调用仅解释有限变异（R²=0.33和0.42），SAS达0.88，Oracle-EFC和Estimated-EFC达0.94，Oracle-EFC/D_task达0.99。匹配预算干预中，提升反馈质量使成功率从0.27升至0.90而原始成本与工具调用不变。混合真实追踪中NRS-EFC/D_task达R²=0.92，前瞻保留集达0.85。

Conclusion: 智能体系统的缩放效果主要不取决于计算总量，而取决于原始预算转化为耐用且任务充分的反馈的效率。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly determine the performance of language-model systems by deciding how models call tools, receive feedback, verify intermediate states, store memory, and revise solutions. Yet current test-time scaling analyses often parameterize this process by raw expenditure -- tokens, tool calls, operations, wall time, or cost -- which does not distinguish useful feedback from redundant or unstable interaction. We introduce \emph{Effective Feedback Compute} (EFC), a trace-level scaling coordinate that credits feedback only when it is informative, valid, non-redundant, and retained for subsequent decisions, and we normalize it by task demand when comparing tasks with different feedback requirements. Across synthetic controllable tasks, executable code tasks, real benchmark traces, held-out splits, and a prospective validation batch, EFC-based coordinates consistently predict failure rates better than raw-compute baselines and a strong multivariate SAS baseline. In controlled scaling, raw tokens and tool calls explain limited variation ($R^2=0.33$ and $0.42$), SAS reaches $0.88$, while Oracle-EFC and Estimated-EFC reach $0.94$ and Oracle-EFC/$D_{\mathrm{task}}$ reaches $0.99$. Matched-budget interventions show that improving feedback quality raises success from $0.27$ to $0.90$ while raw cost and tool calls are fixed. On mixed real traces, NRS-EFC/$D_{\mathrm{task}}$ reaches $R^2=0.92$ while raw compute has near-zero or negative fit, and it remains the best predictor in a prospective holdout ($R^2=0.85$). These results suggest that harness scaling is governed less by how much computation is spent than by how efficiently raw budget is converted into durable, task-sufficient feedback.

</details>


### [154] [Domino: Decoupling Causal Modeling from Autoregressive Drafting in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29707)
*Jianuo Huang,Yaojie Zhang,Qituan Zhang,Hao Lin,Hanlin Xu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Domino提出了一种解耦因果依赖建模与自回归草稿执行的推测解码框架，通过并行草稿主干与轻量级Domino头结合，并在Qwen3上实现最高5.49倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法在草稿质量与生成成本之间存在权衡：自回归草稿器能建模因果依赖但串行开销大，并行草稿器成本低但弱化了块内依赖建模。

Method: 提出Domino框架：(1) 并行草稿主干生成初步草稿分布；(2) 轻量级Domino头通过前缀依赖的因果信息精炼分布；(3) 基础锚定训练课程先强化并行主干，再逐步转向因果校正的最终分布。

Result: 在Qwen3模型上，Transformers后端实现最高5.49倍端到端加速，SGLang服务下实现最高5.8倍吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: 通过解耦策略，Domino在保持高质量草稿的同时大幅降低推理成本，为LLM推理加速提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel with the target model. However, its practical speedup is constrained by the trade-off between draft quality and drafting cost: autoregressive drafters model causal dependencies among draft tokens but incur sequential overhead, while parallel drafters reduce drafting cost but weaken intra-block dependency modeling. In this paper, we propose Domino, a speculative decoding framework that decouples causal dependency modeling from expensive autoregressive draft execution. Domino first uses a parallel draft backbone to produce preliminary draft distributions for the entire block, and then applies a lightweight Domino head to refine them with prefix-dependent causal information. To stabilize teacher-forced causal encoding, we further introduce a base-anchored training curriculum that first strengthens the parallel backbone and then gradually shifts optimization toward the causally corrected final distribution. Experiments on Qwen3 models show that Domino achieves up to \(5.49\times\) end-to-end speedup under the Transformers backend and up to \(5.8\times\) throughput speedup under SGLang serving.

</details>


### [155] [Understanding Safety-Sensitive Expert Behavior in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29708)
*Zhibo Zhang,Yuxi Li,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE大语言模型中安全对齐与路由器驱动的专家专业化之间的关系，发现路由模式主要由主题驱动而非安全行为控制。提出RASET框架，通过对比路由敏感性准则识别安全关键专家并仅对其进行参数高效微调，揭示了MoE模型存在独特的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对MoE LLM中安全对齐与路由专家专业化之间的相互作用理解不足，一种常见直觉认为安全行为可通过将有害请求路由到专门的拒绝专家来控制，但这一假设缺乏充分验证。

Method: 提出RASET框架：(1) 通过对比路由敏感性准则识别安全关键专家；(2) 仅对选中的专家进行参数高效微调，保持模型固有路由行为不变，比路由导向干预带来更小的语义干扰。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 对齐MoE LLM的路由模式主要由主题驱动，而非安全行为；(2) 安全行为可在几乎不改变模型固有路由路径的情况下被改变；(3) RASET有效定位到少数安全关键专家，证明了安全机制集中在少数专家中。

Conclusion: 揭示了MoE模型存在独特的安全风险——安全行为可通过微调少数专家而被绕过，而路由模式基本不变，这凸显了需要专家感知的对齐机制来增强MoE LLM的安全性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs rely on sparse, router-driven expert activation, yet how safety alignment interacts with routed expert specialization remains underexplored. A common intuition is that safety behavior may be controlled by routing harmful requests to distinct refusal-oriented experts. In this work, we provide empirical evidence for a different picture: routing patterns in aligned MoE LLMs are largely topic-driven, while safety behavior can be altered with little change to the model's intrinsic routing path.
  Motivated by this observation, we present **RASET** (**R**outer-**A**gnostic **S**afety-critical **E**xpert **T**uning), a red-teaming framework that probes safety enforcement that is localized in a small subset of experts while preserving the model's intrinsic routing behavior. **RASET** identifies safety-critical experts via a contrastive routing-sensitivity criterion and applies parameter-efficient tuning only to the selected experts, minimizing semantic disruption relative to router-steering interventions. These results reveal a distinct MoE safety risk, highlighting the need for expert-aware alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [156] [Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29711)
*Zhefan Wang,Zhiqiang Guo,Weizhi Ma,Min Zhang,Quanjia Yan,Hengliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合用户记忆与回合上下文的个性化对话满意度评估器，通过事后分数校准提升序一致性与不满检测能力，并构建了PersTurnBench基准，在固定回放状态下实现对不同生成模型的可控比较，无需为每个模型收集新的人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对AI助手的满意度高度个性化，同一回答对不同用户可能截然不同，而现有自动评估方法主要衡量通用回复质量，难以判断某个回复在特定回合是否满足特定用户的需求。

Method: 构建结合紧凑用户记忆与目标回合上下文的满意度评估器，输出满意度分数及不满导向的rationale；引入事后分数校准（post-hoc score calibration）提升评估准确性；构建PersTurnBench基准，在固定回放状态下评估不同生成模型。

Result: 与人工满意度标注的元评估显示，个性化记忆与事后分数校准在序一致性和不满回合检测上优于监督式、检索式和通用LLM-as-a-judge基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提评估器与基准使得研究人员无需为每个候选模型收集新用户反馈，即可在个性化满意度维度上对比不同生成模型。

Abstract: User satisfaction with AI assistants is highly personalized: the same response may satisfy one user but disappoint another depending on what each user expects and what they have asked for before. Existing automatic evaluation methods mostly measure generic response quality, making it difficult to judge whether a response satisfies a user at a specific turn. We study this problem as personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction evaluation. We build a conversation satisfaction evaluator that combines compact user memories with target-turn context to produce satisfaction scores and dissatisfaction-oriented rationales. Meta-evaluation against human satisfaction annotations shows that personalized memory and post-hoc score calibration improve ordinal agreement and dissatisfied-turn detection over supervised, retrieval-based, and generic LLM-as-a-judge baselines. We further introduce PersTurnBench, a personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction benchmark that uses the verified evaluator to assess generation models via replay. By holding the replay state fixed, PersTurnBench enables controlled comparison of generic generation models and memory-augmented personalized systems without new human labels for every candidate model. The evaluator and benchmark let researchers compare candidate generation models on personalized satisfaction without collecting new user feedback for every model.

</details>


### [157] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于证据的事实性验证建模为真假阅读理解任务，通过引导LLM使用明确的应试策略进行高效推理，相比无引导的开放式推理节省80%以上的token使用量，在两项事实性基准上达到竞争性表现，并创下一项新SOTA。此外，通过监督微调和自我修正机制训练小型语言模型取代LLM，在保持低推理成本的同时生成可解释的理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于蕴涵分类器的事实性验证指标需要针对特定数据集调整阈值，而基于LLM的方法通常使用直接提示，未能充分利用LLM的推理能力。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出更高效、更准确的事实性验证方法。

Method: 1) 将基于证据的事实性验证建模为真假阅读理解任务；2) 引导LLM使用明确的应试策略进行高效推理；3) 通过监督微调（SFT）和自我修正机制训练小型语言模型（SLM）替代LLM进行验证。

Result: 相比无引导的开放式推理，token使用量减少80%以上；在两项事实性基准上达到与更昂贵的替代方案竞争的性能，并在一项基准上达到新的SOTA；训练出的SLM在性能上与强基线模型相当，同时推理成本更低并能生成可解释的理由。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法在保证事实性验证性能的同时显著降低了推理成本，并通过SLM训练进一步提升了效率与可解释性，为实际部署提供了可行的方案。

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [158] [Leveraging Routing Dynamics in Mixture-of-Experts Models for Efficient Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29714)
*Aditi Khandelwal,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers,Siva Reddy,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了英语为主的MoE模型在多语言持续预训练中的路由动态，发现早期和中间层路由是语言无关的，语言专业化主要出现在最后几层；基于此提出了一种参数高效的适配策略，仅更新最后MoE层中的语言特定和共享专家，在更新少于2%参数的情况下取得了与微调完整层相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型被广泛用于扩展语言模型，但其在多语言场景下的专家路由行为和适应性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1) 分析多语言持续预训练中MoE模型的路由动态，研究专家使用在不同语言间的变化；2) 提出参数高效的适配策略，仅更新最后MoE层中的语言特定专家和共享专家。

Result: 在MultiBLiMP和Belebele基准上，该方法在更新不到2%参数的情况下，达到了与微调完整最后层相竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 多语言持续预训练中，MoE模型的语言专业化主要出现在最后几层，且语言间的token级词汇重叠程度对路由有重要影响；提出的参数高效适配策略为低资源多语言适配提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely used to scale language models, yet their expert routing behavior and adaptation in a multilingual setting remain underexplored. In this work, we study multilingual routing dynamics during continual pre-training of an English-centric MoE model on a multilingual corpus, analyzing how expert usage varies across languages. We find that continual multilingual pre-training leads to diffused, language-agnostic routing in early and middle layers, with language specialization primarily emerging in the final layers. We also show that token-level vocabulary overlap between languages plays an important role in how languages are routed. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient adaptation strategy that updates language-specific and shared experts in the final MoE layers. Experiments on MultiBLiMP and Belebele show that our method achieves a strong performance-efficiency trade-off, attaining competitive performance relative to fine-tuning complete final layers, while updating less than 2% of the parameters. Overall, our findings provide insights into where and how language specialization emerges in MoEs during continual pre-training and provide practical insights for low-resource multilingual adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/aditi184/moe-routing-adaptation.

</details>


### [159] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UKA是一个无需梯度的主动对话学习框架，通过显式建模用户需求不确定性并整合主动学习到知识获取和响应选择中，利用心智理论不确定性估计机制优先选择能引发更多用户反馈的响应，从而在多轮对话中高效获取与用户对齐的对话知识，显著提升对话质量和用户对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统难以高效获取和泛化对话知识，因为用户需求信号通常微弱、间接且需要通过多轮交互才能消歧，现有方法在处理这种不确定性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出UKA框架，包含：(1) 基于心智理论的不确定性估计机制，用于评估和优先选择能引发更多用户信息的响应；(2) 无梯度的主动对话学习框架，将主动学习同时整合到知识获取与响应选择两个阶段；(3) 在训练时高效探索与用户对齐的对话知识，同时在测试时保持鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个对话基准测试和多种模型架构上的实验表明，UKA在对话质量和用户对齐方面持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模用户需求不确定性并引入主动学习策略，UKA有效弥补了现有情感支持对话系统在知识获取和泛化方面的不足，为多轮交互中的用户对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [160] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HTAM提出了一种层级化过渡注意力记忆框架，通过构建两级层级过渡图（HTG）来组织粗粒度全局方向和细粒度局部策略，从而解决LLM在GPU算子优化中粒度不匹配的问题，在KernelBench上提升了正确率和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based GPU算子优化方法面临粒度不匹配问题：粗粒度提示可复用但难以执行，细粒度记忆虽可操作但扩大搜索空间且模糊优化瓶颈。核心挑战在于以合适的粒度组织优化经验。

Method: 提出HTAM框架，构建两级层级过渡图（HTG），分别组织粗粒度全局方向、细粒度局部策略以及优化步骤间的过渡经验。每个演化步骤中，HTAM根据当前状态和优化历史选择全局方向，检索对应局部策略记忆，指导CUDA代码生成。

Result: 在完整KernelBench基准上，HTAM在正确率、快速求解率和加速比上持续优于LLM基线；后端和Robust-KBench研究显示结构化记忆具有可迁移的收益。

Conclusion: 层级化的粗到细记忆组织方式能有效解决LLM在算子优化中的粒度不匹配问题，提升优化效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


### [161] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Multi-Legal-Bench是首个跨司法管辖区法律NLP基准，覆盖6国、4语系、1.34亿份判决，定义5项任务，评估11个LLM，发现少样本效果跨辖区一致、无单一模型主导、跨语言迁移不遵循语言邻近度、分词器效率对准确率影响不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律NLP基准多为单语言或跨辖区任务不可比，无法进行跨语言比较。亟需一个统一任务定义的跨司法管辖区基准来系统评估LLM在法律领域的跨语言能力。

Method: 构建5×6任务-辖区稀疏矩阵（30格中20格有数据），涵盖乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛6国。定义法庭类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范提取和原因类别预测5项任务。通过AWS Bedrock评估7个前沿LLM的零样本和3样本提示，另加4个小/中型模型（3-12B）进行规模分析。

Result: (1) 任务依赖的少样本效果在乌克兰发现后跨辖区复现；(2) 无单一模型主导，语言排名随任务和辖区变化；(3) 跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言邻近度：UA→FR（罗曼语族，-2.1 pp）优于UA→PL（斯拉夫语族，-13.7 pp），标签集对齐比语系更能预测迁移质量；(4) 分词器效能在2.3倍差异下与跨语言准确率相关性不显著（r=-0.27, p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 跨语言法律NLP中任务设计、标签对齐和模型架构比语言亲缘关系或分词效率更关键。该基准为未来跨司法管辖区法律AI研究提供了标准化评估框架和开放资源。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [162] [AfriScience-MT: Towards Decolonizing Science in Africa through Text Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29741)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Tajuddeen Gwadabe,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,David Ifeoluwa Adelani,Nomonde Khalo,Ibrahim Said Ahmad,Abiodun Modupe,Anina Mumm,Sibusiso Biyela,Michelle Rabie,Johanna Havemann,Marek Rei,Jade Abbott,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AfriScience-MT，一个覆盖6种非洲语言、11个科学领域的平行语料库，用于推动非洲语言的科学机器翻译。研究发现闭源模型（GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）表现最佳，开源模型中微调NLLB-1.3B和TranslateGemma-12B表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲教育及科学传播中殖民地语言的主导地位限制了数以亿计的非洲语言使用者获取和产出科学知识，核心障碍是这些语言缺乏成熟的科学术语体系。

Method: 构建了包含阿姆哈拉语、豪萨语、卢干达语、北索托语、约鲁巴语和祖鲁语6种非洲语言、覆盖11个科学领域的平行语料库AfriScience-MT。由专业翻译人员与科学传播专家合作翻译科学论文的通俗语言摘要，并在必要时创建新术语。随后在零样本、少样本和微调设置下评估了机器翻译系统和大型语言模型。

Result: 闭源模型全面优于开源模型：GPT-5.4和Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在句子级平均COMET得分分别为68.3和68.0，文档级均为48.3。开源模型中，微调NLLB-1.3B句子级达67.3，TranslateGemma-12B在1-shot上下文学习下文档级达44.0。

Conclusion: AfriScience-MT语料库的发布将支持非洲语言科学机器翻译的基准测试和文档级翻译研究，有助于推动非洲语言在科学领域的应用。

Abstract: The dominance of colonial languages in African education and scientific communication limits how hundreds of millions of speakers of African languages access and produce scientific knowledge. A core obstacle is the lack of established scientific terminology in these languages. We introduce AfriScience-MT, a parallel corpus covering six African languages (Amharic, Hausa, Luganda, Northern Sotho, Yorùbá, and isiZulu) across 11 scientific domains. Professional translators, working with expert science communicators, translated plain-language summaries of scientific papers into each target language and created new terms where none existed. We benchmark machine translation systems and large language models in zero-shot, few-shot, and fine-tuned settings. Our results show that closed-source models outperform all open-source models at both the sentence and document levels: GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite lead with average sentence-level COMET scores of 68.3 and 68.0, respectively, and tie at an average document-level COMET of 48.3. Among open systems, fine-tuned NLLB-1.3B reaches 67.3 at the sentence level, and TranslateGemma-12B reaches 44.0 at the document level with 1-shot in-context learning. We release AfriScience-MT to support benchmarking and document-level scientific MT for African languages.

</details>


### [163] [DySem: Uncovering Dynamic Semantic Components via Multilingual Consensus for Calculating Semantic Textual Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29751)
*Kaijie Zheng,Weiqin Wang,Yile Wang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DySem 提出了一种无需训练的新框架，通过多语言共识探索大语言模型中更语义相关的内部组件，并构建文本依赖的联合语义集，在动态、样本特定的低维语义子空间上计算文本相似度，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的语义相似度计算方法通常依赖最后一层固定维度的隐藏状态，但存在两个局限：(1) 最后一层隐藏层编码的是一般知识而非纯粹的语义知识；(2) 隐藏层维度通常很大，引入冗余和噪声，不利于语义表示。

Method: 提出 DySem，一种无需训练的框架。通过多语言共识挖掘大语言模型中更语义相关的内部组件，构建文本依赖的联合语义集（text-dependent joint semantic set），在动态的、样本特定的共享语义子集上计算相似度，突破静态表示空间的限制。

Result: 在多种大语言模型上的大量实验表明，DySem 在保持更低计算维度的同时，一致优于近期基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过探索模型内部更语义相关的组件并采用动态样本特定维度，可以有效提升语义相似度计算的性能，同时降低计算开销。

Abstract: Calculating semantic textual similarity is a foundational task in natural language processing. Current large language models (LLMs) based methods typically rely on extracting last-layer hidden states with fixed dimensions to compute similarity for every text pairs. We argue that this paradigm is suffer from two limitations: (i) The last hidden layer encodes more general knowledge rather than just semantic knowledge, making it suboptimal for semantic similarity computation; (ii) The hidden layer dimensions of LLMs are generally very large, which introduces some redundancy and noise for representing semantics. In this work, we propose DySem, a novel training-free framework that investigates more semantic-related internal components of LLMs via multilingual consensus, and shifts away from static representation spaces in favor of dynamic, sample-specific semantic dimensions by constructing text-dependent joint semantic set and computes similarity over this shared dimensional subset. Extensive experiments across various LLMs show that our method consistently outperforms recent baselines while maintaining lower dimensions for similarity calculation. The code is released at https://github.com/szu-tera/DySem.

</details>


### [164] [ActTraitBench: Quantifying the Knowledge-Decision Gap in Large Language Models via Human-Grounded Behavioral Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29791)
*Yutong Yang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ActTraitBench，一个基于人类数据的评估框架，用于测量大语言模型（LLM）在人格一致性方面的知识-决策差距（G_KD），并引入Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA)方法以缩小该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在测量LLM人格一致性时存在结构效度不足、多维度纠缠以及基于LLM评估的分布偏差等问题，无法准确量化模型在自我报告与行为决策之间的不对称性。

Method: ActTraitBench基于实证人类数据建立心理测量学维度与行为范式之间的一一映射，并通过分位数映射分布校准（Quantile Mapping）使LLM裁判的评分分布与人类规范对齐；CoCA则是一种即插即用的推理时干预方法，通过认知对齐链改善一致性。

Result: 在14个主流LLM上的实验表明，存在普遍的知识-决策不对称性：更大、更强大的模型尽管自我报告高度一致，但行为偏差更为显著。CoCA能有效改善具备推理能力的前沿模型的一致性，但在较小型架构上暴露了明显的能力限制。

Conclusion: LLM存在系统性的知识-决策差距，且模型能力增强反而加剧行为偏差；CoCA可在推理强的模型上部分缓解该问题，但小型模型的局限性难以突破。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can convincingly simulate personas in explicit self-reports, they often deviate in implicit behavioral decisions, revealing a substantial Knowledge-Decision Gap ($G_{\text{KD}}$). Existing benchmarks struggle to measure this asymmetry due to limited construct validity, multi-dimensional entanglement, and distributional biases in LLM-based evaluation. To address these issues, we propose ActTraitBench, a human-grounded evaluation framework for measuring personality consistency in LLMs. Grounded in empirical human data, ActTraitBench establishes one-to-one mappings between psychometric facets and behavioral paradigms, and applies a Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping procedure to align LLM-judge score distributions with human norms. Experiments on 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a pervasive knowledge-decision asymmetry, where larger and more capable models often exhibit stronger behavioral divergence despite highly consistent self-reports. To mitigate this gap, we further introduce the Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA), a plug-and-play inference-time intervention that improves alignment in reasoning-capable frontier models while exposing clear capability limitations in smaller architectures.

</details>


### [165] [Metric-Dependent Annotation Saturation for Learning from Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29797)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过ChaosNLI数据集的100人标注分布，发现NLI模型对标注不一致性的捕捉能力依赖于评估指标：熵相关性需要20-50个标注者才能收敛，而KL散度在10个标注者时即饱和。软标签优于标签平滑，且该优势跨架构和领域成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未系统探讨标注预算（标注者数量）与评估指标之间的依赖关系，且软标签与标签平滑在捕捉标注不一致性上的差异未被充分量化。

Method: 在ChaosNLI（每项100个独立标注）上对DeBERTa和RoBERTa模型进行微调，比较不同N值下软标签与标签平滑的熵相关性和KL散度，并在内容安全领域进行跨域验证。

Result: 熵相关性需N=20-50收敛，KL散度在N=10时饱和（达87-95%改进）；软标签熵相关性r=0.643，标签平滑仅r=0.45-0.49；该差异源于平滑无法区分模糊与清晰样本；结果跨架构和领域可复制。

Conclusion: 标注预算应根据目标评估指标而非统一设定，软标签能有效捕捉标注不一致性中的信号。

Abstract: When annotators disagree on a label, the disagreement itself carries signal -- and the number of annotators needed to capture it depends on the evaluation metric. We fine-tune NLI models on label distributions subsampled from ChaosNLI, a dataset providing 100 independent annotator judgments per item, and identify metric-dependent saturation. In our 3-class NLI setting, entropy correlation -- whether the model identifies which items elicit disagreement -- requires N ~ 20-50 annotators to converge, while distributional match (KL divergence) saturates by N ~ 10 (87-95% of improvement across five model seeds). This finding rests on a prior observation: soft labels carry item-specific signal that label smoothing cannot replicate. Across five smoothing intensities, entropy correlation clusters at r ~ 0.45-0.49, while soft labels reach r = 0.643 (p < 0.001); per-item analysis traces this gap to smoothing's inability to distinguish ambiguous items from clear ones. The soft-label advantage replicates across two architectures (DeBERTa, RoBERTa), a non-NLI-pretrained baseline, and an exploratory cross-domain evaluation on content safety. These results suggest that annotation budgets should be informed by the target evaluation metric rather than set uniformly.

</details>


### [166] [Nine Judges, Two Effective Votes: Correlated Errors Undermine LLM Evaluation Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29800)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM评审团由多个模型组成，但实验发现9个前沿LLM仅提供约2个独立投票的信息量，因模型间高度相关，评审团准确率比独立投票理想值低8-22个百分点，增加法官数量或改进聚合算法均无法弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM评审团（LLM-as-a-judge panels）中多样化模型是否真的能带来更可靠的评估，量化其信息价值与独立投票理想之间的差距，并验证增加法官数量或改进聚合算法能否弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 构建框架使用Kish有效样本量（n_eff）和Condorcet零模型衡量评审团的信息价值；在3个自然语言推理数据集上测试9个来自7个模型家族的LLM；通过多种提示变体、温度参数、思维链推理及偏好任务（RewardBench）验证结果的鲁棒性。

Result: 9个法官仅提供约2个独立投票的信息量；约3/4的名义独立性因模型在相同项目上犯相同错误而丧失；评审团实际准确率比独立投票理想值低8-22个百分点；最佳单模型在所有条件下匹配或优于完整评审团；增加法官数量或使用更智能的聚合算法（即使接触正确答案）最多只能缩小11%的差距。

Conclusion: LLM评审团的瓶颈在于法官之间高度相关而非聚合算法，规模化评审团无法替代真正独立的评估，亟需开发独立的评估方法。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge panels aggregate votes from multiple models, with the expectation that diverse models yield more reliable evaluations. We develop a framework to measure the true informational value of such panels and quantify how far their reliability falls short of the independent-voting ideal. Testing a panel of 9 frontier LLMs from 7 model families on three natural language inference datasets (each with 100 human annotations per item), we find that the 9 judges effectively provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information. Roughly three-quarters of the panel's nominal independence is lost because the models make the same mistakes on the same items. The consequences are stark: the panel's actual accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of what independent voting would achieve, and the best single judge matches or outperforms the full panel across all conditions. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps -- established methods close at most 11% of this gap, even with access to the correct answers. We quantify these findings using the Kish effective sample size (n_eff) and a Condorcet null model, and show the deficit is robust across prompt variants, temperatures, chain-of-thought reasoning, and a pairwise preference task (RewardBench). The bottleneck is correlated judges, not the aggregation algorithm, implying that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation.

</details>


### [167] [Data filtering methods for training language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29807)
*Egor Shevchenko,Elena Bruches*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography两种自动标签错误检测方法在三个俄语文本分类语料上的效果，发现方法有效性高度依赖数据集特征：在大规模低噪声语料上过滤无效，而在小规模高噪声语料上Confident Learning显著提升F1-macro，两种方法均优于随机去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量对机器学习模型效果至关重要，标签错误普遍存在于基准数据集中，会引入训练噪声并降低模型泛化能力。现有研究缺乏对自动标签错误检测方法在俄语文本分类任务上的系统性比较分析。

Method: 在三个俄语文本分类语料（ru_emotion_e-culture 49,123例、RuCoLA 8,524例、TERRa 2,337例）上，使用预训练rubert-base-cased模型微调，分别应用Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography检测标签错误，并通过随机去除等量样本的对照实验验证过滤的有效性。

Result: Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography的有效性强烈依赖数据集特征：大规模低噪声语料上过滤不提升性能，小规模高噪声语料上Confident Learning取得显著F1-macro提升；Dataset Cartography更保守，去除样本更少；两种方法均优于随机去除。

Conclusion: 自动标签错误检测方法的效果并非普适，需根据数据集规模与噪声水平选择合适方法；Confident Learning更适合小规模高噪声场景，而Dataset Cartography行为更保守；定向去除策略比随机去除更有意义。

Abstract: Data quality is a critical factor in the effectiveness of machine learning models. Label errors, present even in widely used benchmarks, introduce noise into training data and reduce model generalization. In this work, we conduct a comparative analysis of two automatic label error detection methods - Confident Learning and Dataset Cartography - on three Russian text classification corpora of varying size, number of classes, and domain: ru_emotion_e-culture (49,123 examples, emotion classification), RuCoLA (8,524 examples, linguistic acceptability), and TERRa (2,337 examples, textual entailment recognition). We use the pre-trained rubert-base-cased model fine-tuned on each corpus. To verify the meaningfulness of filtering, we conduct control experiments with random removal of an equivalent number of examples. Results show that the effectiveness of both methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics: on large corpora with low noise levels, filtering does not improve performance, while on small datasets with high noise, Confident Learning achieves a significant F1-macro improvement. Dataset Cartography demonstrates more conservative behavior, removing fewer examples. Across all corpora, targeted removal by both methods outperforms random removal, confirming the meaningfulness of the approaches.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu,Zhen Zeng,Feng Li,Xinjian Gao,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有多模态知识编辑方法虽能修正多模态大模型中的错误知识，但存在因果错位（编辑仅限于特定样本）和特征纠缠（意外修改无关但关联的信息）两大缺陷。为此提出局部解耦知识编辑（LDKE），通过快速定位模块识别关键层、解耦分类器区分相关/无关输入，实现精确且泛化的编辑，在多项基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MKE方法存在两个关键缺陷限制了其有效性：一是Causal Misalignment，编辑仅作用于目标事实对，无法泛化到逻辑相关的查询；二是Feature Entanglement，编辑会意外地篡改虽视觉或语义上关联但无关的信息。这些缺陷导致编辑不够精确和泛化。

Method: 提出LDKE框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Fast Localization模块，快速定位并更新与目标事实相关的关键模型层；2) Disentanglement Classifier，对输入进行路由分类，将目标相关输入导向已更新的层，无关输入则保持原状，从而实现目标信息与非目标信息的解耦。

Result: 在多个基准测试和多模态大语言模型上，LDKE在传播编辑到相关上下文方面表现优越，同时保持高度的局部性（即不影响无关信息）。

Conclusion: LDKE通过定位与解耦的双重机制，有效解决了现有MKE方法在泛化性和局部性之间的权衡问题，为多模态知识编辑提供了一种更精确、更可控的范式。

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [169] [EvoRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric-Driven RL for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29847)
*Xin Guan,Xiaomeng Hu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Bo Zhang,Zijian Li,Pengjun Xie,Bo Liu,Jiuxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EvoRubric，一种单策略协同进化强化学习框架，通过统一响应生成与评估标准生成策略，并引入多级验证机制（元验证器、零方差剪枝、留一法同行共识），在医疗、写作和科学领域超越传统静态和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的RL方法依赖静态人工标注或昂贵的外部专有模型进行动态更新，前者导致策略滞后，后者成本高昂，亟需一种无需外部依赖的动态评分标准生成方法。

Method: 提出EvoRubric，将响应生成和评分标准生成统一为单一参数化策略，动态交替扮演Reasoner和Rubric Generator；引入多级验证管道（元验证器、零方差剪枝、留一法同行共识）防止奖励破解，并将验证后的标准存入记忆池，提供密集多目标奖励进行协同优化。

Result: 在医疗、写作和科学领域的实验中，EvoRubric一致优于传统静态和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法；初始化时加入专家标注先验可进一步发现新的判别维度，超越仅依赖静态专家标注的性能。

Conclusion: EvoRubric通过单策略协同进化框架有效解决了开放式生成中对齐的奖励信号匮乏问题，消除了对外部评分标准生成器的依赖，同时保持了对人类专家先验的兼容性，为LLM在非可验证领域的对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly advanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in verifiable domains, but aligning models for open-ended generation remains profoundly challenging due to the lack of definitive rewards. Current rubric-based RL methods mitigate this by employing explicit criteria; however, they rely heavily on static, human-annotated rubrics that inevitably cause policy lag, or expensive external proprietary models for dynamic updates. In this paper, we propose EvoRubric, a novel single-policy co-evolutionary RL framework that eliminates the reliance on static criteria and on external rubric generators. By unifying response generation and rubric generation under a single parameterized policy, EvoRubric dynamically alternates between a Reasoner and a Rubric Generator. To prevent reward hacking and ensure the reliability of generated signals, we introduce a multi-level verification pipeline featuring a meta-verifier, zero-variance pruning, and a Leave-One-Out peer consensus mechanism. Validated criteria are dynamically archived into a memory pool, yielding dense, multi-objective rewards to continuously co-optimize both roles. Extensive experiments across Medical, Writing, and Science domains demonstrate that EvoRubric consistently outperforms traditional static and external-LLM-driven alignment methods. Notably, our framework is compatible with human-expert priors. When initialized with expert-annotated rubrics, EvoRubric can further uncover novel, discriminative dimensions, achieving better performance than relying solely on static expert annotations.

</details>


### [170] [Towards Verifiable Multimodal Deep Research: A Multi-Agent Harness for Interleaved Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29861)
*Chenghao Zhang,Guanting Dong,Yufan Liu,Tong Zhao,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Ptah，一个多智能体框架，用于生成可验证的多模态深度研究报告，通过规划、研究和写作阶段以及视觉工作记忆机制，在多个基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM驱动的深度研究大多局限于文本报告，缺乏可信的多模态证据交织能力，且难以验证开放式合成结果。需要一种能够生成具有视觉证据、可验证的多模态深度研究报告的方法。

Method: 提出了Ptah多智能体框架，包含三个关键阶段：(1) 规划阶段：构建视觉感知的计划；(2) 研究阶段：收集基于声明的证据，并在视觉工作记忆中维护来源对齐的图像；(3) 写作阶段：通过声明式多模态工具组合生成报告。引入验证器智能体作为验收函数，确保事实基础、引用忠实性和跨模态一致性。同时提出PtahEval评估协议，增加图像级和呈现级评估。

Result: 在深度研究基准上的实验表明，Ptah生成的报告比强基线更可靠、视觉信息更丰富、更便于人类使用。

Conclusion: Ptah框架通过多智能体协作和视觉工作记忆机制，有效解决了多模态深度研究中的可验证性问题，生成了更高质量的人类面向报告。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced autonomous agents from deep search, which retrieves concise factual answers, to deep research, which synthesizes scattered evidence into long-form reports. However, verifiable multimodal deep research remains challenging due to open-ended synthesis without deterministic ground truth and the need to interleave textual arguments with visual evidence. We propose \textsc{Ptah}, a multi-agent harness for interleaved report generation. \textsc{Ptah} orchestrates the lifecycle from user query to rendered web report through planning, research, and writing stages, where specialized agents construct visual-aware plans, collect claim-grounded evidence, maintain source-aligned images in a \textit{Visual Working Memory}, and compose reports through declarative multimodal tool use. A verifier agent serves as the harness's acceptance function, enforcing factual grounding, citation fidelity, and cross-modal consistency throughout the workflow. We further introduce \textsc{Ptah}Eval, an evaluation protocol that augments existing benchmarks with image-level and presentation-level assessments. Experiments on deep research benchmarks show that \textsc{Ptah} produces more reliable, visually informative, and usable human-facing multimodal reports than strong baselines.

</details>


### [171] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao,Ziwei Zhang,Chuanyue Yu,Xingcheng Fu,Qingyun Sun,Runhua Xu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRITIC-R1框架，通过将RAG批评重构为结构化错误诊断问题，并利用强化学习（GRPO）训练批评模型，结合保守判断对齐（CJA）和诊断质量对齐（DQA）两种奖励函数，在五个QA基准上持续提升RAG答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法仍存在幻觉和推理错误；外部批评者提供的反馈粗粒度、弱结构化、过度干预且不可靠，限制了修正效果。

Method: 1) 将RAG批评建模为多维度错误诊断问题（裁决、错误位置、推理分析、修复生成）；2) 设计CJA奖励校准高维判断、抑制过度干预，DQA奖励通过门控机制提升细粒度诊断；3) 使用GRPO强化学习结合外部LLM教师的过程级监督训练批评模型。

Result: 在五个QA基准上，CRITIC-R1持续优于强RAG基线，显著提升答案质量。

Conclusion: 结构化的批评框架结合强化学习的奖励设计能有效缓解RAG中的幻觉和推理错误，提升系统整体可靠性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines.
  Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/critic-r1-FCB0

</details>


### [172] [Internal Representation, Not Clinical Knowledge: Where Apparent LLM Triage Failures Originate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29889)
*David Fraile Navarro,Berardino Como,Jialei Sheng,Soundariya Ananthan,Shlomo Berkovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在分诊任务中，输出格式（自由文本 vs 多项选择）导致性能差异的根本原因。通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析Gemma 3和Qwen3-8B模型，发现临床表征在两种格式下保持一致，但多项选择决策token处医学特征静默，而格式特征驱动决策。多项选择惩罚在结构和自然语言输入下均存在，且差距主要由相邻答案的偏离而非知识缺失导致。结论：失败源于输出格式而非临床表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明消费者LLM在约束性多项选择输出中存在高低于分诊率（under-triage），但相同病例在自由文本输出中评分不同。该现象引发关键问题：输出格式的改变是否改变了模型的临床表征，还是仅改变了从保留表征到答案的映射。作者试图厘清这一根本性问题。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征分析Gemma 3 4B/12B IT和Qwen3-8B模型。通过三种独立方法交叉验证：自然语言自动编码器口头化、决策token logit归因、以及顶部特征表征。同时进行行为实验，包括结构与自然语言输入下的多项选择惩罚反转测试、选项顺序洗牌排除位置偏差、以及误差类型分解。

Result: 相同医学特征在两种格式共有的临床叙述上激活，但在多项选择决策token处所有病例中均静默。多种方法一致证明：脚手架和格式特征（而非医学特征）驱动决策logits。多项选择惩罚在结构和自然语言输入下均反转存在，选项顺序洗牌排除位置偏差，且差距主要由相邻字母等级的偏离（off-by-one decision）主导，而非知识缺失。

Conclusion: LLM在临床分诊任务中因输出格式导致的性能差距，其根源在于输出格式本身（多项选择决策token处医学特征被抑制），而非模型的临床知识表征受损。这提示改进输出接口设计可能比增强医学知识训练更能缓解此类问题。

Abstract: Patient-voiced clinical-triage benchmarks report high under-triage rates for consumer LLMs for constrained multiple-choice output, yet the same cases score differently with free-text. We ask whether output format changes the model's \emph{clinical representation} or only the mapping from a preserved representation to an answer. Using sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features in Gemma 3 4B/12B IT and Qwen3-8B, we find the same medical features fire on the shared clinical narrative under both formats but go {silent} at the multiple-choice decision token in all the cases at every model. Three independent methods (natural-language autoencoder verbalization, decision-token logit attribution, and top-feature characterization) agree that scaffold and format features, but not medical features, drive the decision logits. Behaviorally, the multiple-choice penalty inverts under both structured and natural-language input, option-order shuffle rules out positional bias, and the gap is dominated by off-by-one decision (the model picks an adjacent acuity letter to the gold answer) rather than knowledge failure. Thus, the failure originates in the output format and not in the clinical representation.

</details>


### [173] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter,Haiyue Song,Hour Kaing,Jin Tei,Hideki Tanaka,Masao Utiyama,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ExCAM，一个可解释的文化意识评估指标，用于识别、评分和解释指令-输出对中的文化错误。同时构建了ExCAM40k数据集（整合九个现有基准并加入合成错误），在平衡测试集上达到80%的检测准确率，为细粒度、可解释的自由文本文化评估开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型的文化意识对确保生成文本的公平性和应用的全球泛化能力至关重要，但现有基准依赖耗时昂贵的人工标注，且针对自由文本的文化评估基准稀缺，评估机制陈旧。

Method: 提出ExCAM（可解释文化意识指标），用于识别、评级和解释指令-输出对中的文化错误；并构建ExCAM40k数据集，该数据集整合九个现有基准并增强合成错误，用于训练和评估ExCAM。

Result: 在平衡测试集上，ExCAM相比包括GPT-5在内的多个基线模型，实现了最高错误检测率，准确率达到80%。

Conclusion: ExCAM为自由文本的细粒度、可解释文化评估开辟了新路径，解决了现有方法依赖人工标注和评估机制陈旧的局限。

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [174] [Causal Interventions on Continuous Variables: A Case Study on Verb Bias in Steering Vectors for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29971)
*Zhenghao Herbert Zhou,R. Thomas McCoy,Robert Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对语言模型表示进行连续变量因果干预的方法，通过定位低维方向并编辑表征向量来实现反事实编辑。以动词偏好（verb bias）这一连续变量为例，证明该变量在大语言模型中被因果性地编码在引导向量中，并系统性地影响句法结构选择。同时发现引导向量编码了可用于上下文学习的误差信号，但这些信号在下游生成中并未被因果性地利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型因果干预研究主要针对离散特征（如语法数），但语言模型也需要处理具有渐变性质的连续特征。作者希望将因果干预方法扩展到连续变量上，以更全面地理解语言模型的内部表征机制。

Method: 提出一种针对连续变量的因果干预方法：将激活向量与标记的连续目标变量配对，定位该变量的低维方向，然后利用该方向将向量编辑为反事实的目标值。以心理语言学中研究充分的动词偏好（verb bias）作为测试案例。

Result: 1) 动词偏好在大语言模型的引导向量中被因果性地编码，反事实编辑能够系统性改变下游的结构偏好。2) 引导向量编码了可用于驱动上下文学习中误差驱动更新行为的误差信号，但这些信号在下游生成中并未被因果性地使用。

Conclusion: 因果干预方法可以扩展到连续变量，但将连续变量与上下文学习相连接仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Causal interventions in language model representations have largely targeted discrete features, like grammatical number. However, language models must also make use of features that are graded. We introduce a method for causal intervention on continuous variables: given activation vectors paired with a graded target variable, we localize a low-dimensional direction for that variable and use this direction to edit a vectors toward counterfactual target values. We apply this method to a continuous feature that is well-studied in psycholinguistics, namely verb bias (which reflects which syntactic structures tend to follow a given verb). We show that verb bias is causally represented in steering vectors extracted from large language models: counterfactual edits to verb bias systematically shift downstream structural preferences. Verb bias has also previously been linked to in-context learning; in further analyses, we find that steering vectors encode error signals that could drive the error-driven update behavior seen in in-context learning but that these aspects of the steering vectors are not causally used in downstream production. Overall, these results show causal interventions can be applied to continuous variables, though connecting continuous variables to in-context learning remains a challenge.

</details>


### [175] [Adapting Multilingual Embedding Models to Turkish via Cross-Lingual Tokenizer Surgery and Offline Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29992)
*M. Ali Bayram,Banu Diri,Savaş Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 embeddingmagibu-200m，一个土耳其语句子嵌入模型，通过三阶段高效适配流程（多语言分词器优化、嵌入表初始化、离线蒸馏），在单 GPU 上约 4 小时训练完成，模型参数量 200M，在 STSbTR 上超越 300M 教师模型，在 TR-MTEB 上取得 63.9% 平均分，实现了优越的性能-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土耳其语 BERT 编码器受限于 512 token 上下文窗口，且缺乏专门针对土耳其语优化的高性能句子嵌入模型。同时，完整预训练成本高昂，需要一种高效适配方法。

Method: 三阶段适配流程：(1) 通过频率分析从 40 语言语料库构建土耳其语优化的多语言分词器（131,072 词表）；(2) 克隆教师嵌入模型，保留 Transformer 骨干权重，通过均值组合 token 映射初始化新词表嵌入表；(3) 在平衡的 40 语言 Wikipedia 语料上，使用余弦相似度目标从预计算的教师向量进行离线嵌入蒸馏，避免在线教师推理。

Result: 模型约 200M 参数，单 GPU 约 4 小时训练，成本 $5-$20。STSbTR 上 Pearson/Spearman 相关系数 77.55%/77.45%，超越教师模型（73.84%/72.92%）。TR-MTEB（26 任务）平均分 63.9%，在 26 个模型中排名第 7，参数量比教师少 33%。

Conclusion: 提出的三阶段高效适配流程能够以极低的计算成本（单 GPU、4 小时、$5-$20）获得超越更大教师模型的土耳其语句子嵌入性能，为低资源语言嵌入模型的开发提供了可复现的实用范式。

Abstract: Sentence embeddings are a foundational component for semantic search, clustering, classification, and retrieval-augmented generation. This paper presents embeddingmagibu-200m, a Turkish-focused sentence embedding model that produces 768-dimensional L2-normalized vectors and supports an 8,192-token context window, far exceeding the 512-token limit of earlier BERT-based Turkish encoders. Instead of full pretraining, an efficient three-stage adaptation pipeline is introduced: (1) construct a Turkish-optimized multilingual tokenizer with a 131,072 vocabulary by pruning redundant tokens from the teacher's vocabulary and incorporating multilingual tokens via frequency analysis on a 40-language corpus, (2) clone a teacher embedding model while preserving transformer backbone weights and initializing a compatible embedding table for the new vocabulary via mean-composition token mapping, and (3) perform offline embedding distillation from precomputed teacher vectors using a cosine similarity objective over a balanced 40-language Wikipedia corpus. The resulting student model contains approximately 200M parameters and trains in roughly four hours on a single GPU by avoiding online teacher inference during training, at a total cost of $5-$20. Empirically, Pearson/Spearman correlations of 77.55%/77.45% are obtained on STSbTR, surpassing the 300M-parameter teacher model (73.84%/72.92%). On TR-MTEB (26 tasks), a mean score of 63.9% is achieved (7th out of 26 models), providing a competitive cost-quality trade-off with 33% fewer parameters than the teacher. To facilitate reproducibility and downstream use, all artifacts are released including model weights, tokenizer files, precomputed embedding datasets, and open-source cloning and distillation tooling.

</details>


### [176] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在性能剖析（LPP）框架，通过从隐藏激活和输出分布中提取任务无关的诊断指标，从基准中心评估转向状态中心内在评估，揭示LLM的尺度无关特性、隐藏脆弱性及架构敏感签名，并在8个0.5B-14B模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评估存在数据污染、任务范围窄、与真实可靠性脱节等问题，仅关注模型输出什么（what），而非如何加工信息、校准不确定性或组织内部知识（how），因此需要一种互补的内在评估方法。

Method: 构建Latent Performance Profiling（LPP）框架，从模型的隐藏激活和输出分布中提取一组标量度量，定义潜在表征和动力学的任务无关诊断指标，揭示尺度无关且架构敏感的特性。

Result: 在0.5B-14B范围的8个LLM上的实证分析表明，基准分数相近的模型可展现出截然不同的潜在剖面（如熵或适应性差异）；基于这些洞察设计的合成探针与内在度量对齐，同时与排行榜偏差解耦。

Conclusion: 建议将LPP与基准报告相结合，以获得对模型行为更深层、可解释的理解，从而支持更可靠的模型选择、安全评估和超越表面准确率的评价。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于行为策略诱导度量的Mirror-Prox时序差分方法STHTD-MP，其在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替代协方差度量，理论分析和数值实验表明该方法在合适条件下具有更小的均值收缩因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度时序差分方法的实际性能受辅助变量度量几何的强烈影响，现有Mirror-Prox TD方法通常使用特征协方差度量，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息能提供更具信息性的更新几何，因此需要设计一种利用行为策略信息改进度量几何的新方法。

Method: 提出STHTD-MP方法，在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换协方差度量；对原始变量和辅助变量使用单一学习率，应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤处理混合鞍点算子；在标准随机逼近假设下进行收敛性分析。

Result: 行为诱导度量正定时联合均值系统Hurwitz稳定；通过Lyapunov论证有界性成立；ODE方法保证随机递归收敛；导出投影oracle遍历间隙界；与GTD2-MP的确定性Mirror-Prox误差矩阵谱半径精确比较显示，当行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何时STHTD-MP具有更小的均值收缩因子；两状态、随机游走、Boyan Chain基准验证了该条件，Baird反例是严格假设失效的奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP通过引入行为策略信息改进度量几何，在理论上保证了收敛性，在数值上展示了比GTD2-MP更优的均值收缩性能，适用于满足严格假设的强化学习离策略预测问题。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [178] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CCT是一个306M参数的架构，通过在预训练GPT-2 Small上添加基于范畴论和认知科学的组件，在WikiText-103上实现了21.27的验证困惑度，相比微调后的GPT-2 Small基线（24.19）降低了2.92 PPL（12%相对提升）。消融实验证实84%的提升来自GT-Full（单纯复形消息传递），首次在306M参数规模上验证了单纯复形消息传递能改善语言模型困惑度。论文还提出了结构/一致性区分（structure/consistency distinction）的经验模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索范畴论和认知科学启发的组件是否能提升预训练语言模型的性能，并验证单纯复形消息传递这一拓扑方法在大规模语言模型中的有效性。

Method: 在GPT-2 Small骨干上添加基于范畴论和认知科学的组件（包括GT-Full单纯复形消息传递、PrecisionWeightedPP等），在WikiText-103上以匹配步数（215,000步）、匹配数据和优化器设置进行微调，并进行消融实验和负结果分析。

Result: CCT达到21.27验证困惑度，优于GPT-2 Small基线的24.19（2.92 PPL降低，12%相对提升）。消融实验显示GT-Full贡献了84%的提升（2.45 PPL）。三个负结果（sheaf smoothing、adjunction round-trip、curvature regularization）表明一致性类先验无效。

Conclusion: 首次提供消融验证的证据表明单纯复形消息传递能在306M参数规模上改善语言模型困惑度；提出结构/一致性区分经验模式：增加新拓扑的范畴先验提升语言建模，而强制执行一致性恒等式的先验则无效。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [179] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成的论文评审与人类评审的一致性，以及作者利用LLM评审迭代修改论文以提升评分的效果。实验表明两者一致性有限且因提示词和模型而异，且利用LLM评审进行“游戏”可使至多35%的论文获得显著分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被用于论文评审（甚至被官方试点），作者也可能利用LLM修改论文。现有研究缺乏对这两种LLM使用场景的联合评估，特别是作者“针对性改进”可能导致的评审偏差未被充分探讨。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文，设计实验从评审者和作者两个角度评估：1) 比较LLM生成评审与人类评审的一致性（不同提示词和模型）；2) 模拟作者根据LLM评审反馈进行迭代修改（draft-revise流程），观察整体评分变化。

Result: 1) LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限，最佳情况下尚可；2) 一致性在不同提示词和模型间差异显著；3) 作者利用LLM评审迭代修改可在特定场景有效，至多35%的论文获得统计上显著的整体评分提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审存在被“游戏”的风险，作者可通过针对性修改提升评分，这可能导致评审公平性受损，需引起学术社区的关注。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [180] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型的概念擦除任务，本文提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法。该方法将编辑型擦除从几何视角重新表述为乘法参数更新，通过闭式解推导的逐层正交变换实现精确概念擦除，同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何，并引入子空间级目标处理多概念擦除冲突。实验表明OCE在单/多概念擦除上优于现有方法，可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编辑型概念擦除方法依赖加法参数更新，而概念语义主要取决于神经元方向而非幅度，整体生成能力依赖神经元角度几何。加法更新本质上耦合了方向、幅度和角度几何，不可避免地造成概念擦除与生成性能之间的干扰。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）：(1) 将编辑型擦除重新表述为几何视角下的乘法参数更新；(2) 通过闭式解推导逐层正交变换，实现精确概念擦除的同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何；(3) 引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操纵，解决多概念擦除中的冲突约束问题。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上，OCE在概念擦除效果和非目标保持能力上均优于现有方法，能够在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: 通过将编辑型概念擦除从加法更新范式转向乘法正交变换，OCE有效解决了加法更新中方向、幅度和角度几何耦合导致的干扰问题，实现了更精确、高效且可扩展的概念擦除方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [181] [Frontier LLM-based agents can overcome the ontology curation bottleneck for natural phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28965)
*James P. Balhoff,Hilmar Lapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种前沿大语言模型（LLMs）作为“智能策展人”在自由文本表型描述到本体术语的注释任务中的表现，发现所有智能体均达到甚至超过原始研究中人类策展人的一致性水平，并大幅优于传统的NLP工具Semantic CharaParser。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表型注释是将自由文本表型描述链接到本体术语的关键步骤，对跨研究整合比较形态学数据至关重要。然而，该过程高度依赖训练有素的人类专家，劳动密集且难以规模化，成为领域发展的主要瓶颈。因此，探索自动化或半自动化的高效注释方法具有重要研究价值。

Method: 使用来自Anthropic和OpenAI的五个前沿托管LLM作为“智能策展人”，为每个模型提供源出版物PDF、原始人类策展人使用的注释指南、四个项目本体（UBERON、PATO、BSPO、GO）以及验证脚本，构建自包含工作空间。以Dahdul等人（2018）建立的金标准为基准，评估这些智能体在实体-质量（EQ）注释任务上的表现，并与原始研究中三位人类策展人及Semantic CharaParser NLP工具进行对比。

Result: 所有五个LLM智能体的表现均落在原始研究中三位训练有素的人类策展人的策展间变异性范围内；最佳智能体的性能接近但未达到最佳人类策展人水平；所有智能体在所有四项指标上均显著优于Semantic CharaParser工具。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM作为智能策展人能够达到与人类专家相当的表型注释一致性水平，显著优于传统NLP工具，有望在保持高质量的同时实现表型注释流程的规模化扩展。

Abstract: Linking free-text phenotype descriptions to ontology terms, typically referred to as phenotype annotation, is essential for the cross-study integration of comparative morphological data. This labor intensive process has heavily relied on highly trained human experts, which makes it challenging to scale and thus a key bottleneck. Dahdul et al. (2018) established a Gold Standard (GS) of Entity-Quality (EQ) annotations across seven phylogenetic studies and used it to evaluate three human curators and the Semantic CharaParser NLP tool with ontology-based semantic similarity metrics; they reported that machine-human consistency was significantly lower than inter-curator (human-human) consistency. Here we revisit that benchmark with five frontier hosted LLMs from Anthropic and OpenAI, each operating as an "agentic curator" within a self-contained workspace that supplies the source publication PDF, the same annotation guide used by the original human curators, the four project ontologies (UBERON, PATO, BSPO, GO), and a validation script. Evaluated against the same Gold Standard, every agent fell within the range of inter-curator variability of the three trained human biocurators of the original study; the best performing agents approached but did not reach the best performing human curator. Agents substantially outperformed Semantic CharaParser on all four metrics.

</details>


### [182] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VFEAgent，一个端到端多智能体系统，通过多模态视觉-语言流水线和验证优先的代码合成框架，直接从输入图像和问题描述自动化完成有限元分析建模与仿真，在多种工程力学场景中优于现有LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析工作流程复杂且严重依赖领域专家经验，现有结合大语言模型的方案在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限，亟需一种能自动化完成完整FEA流程的方法。

Method: 提出VFEAgent系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多模态视觉-语言多智能体流水线，采用ReAct驱动推理从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规格；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成自调试和回退机制以确保代码可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景中，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究验证了自动化完整FEA工作流程的可行性，该框架有潜力将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>
